0532 — kettlebell figure 8

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0532 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

You pass a kettlebell in a figure 8 pattern around and between your legs. It trains the core, hips and grip while keeping you moving.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0532.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0532.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0532.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kettlebell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0532.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand. 0532.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0532.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pass the kettlebell between your legs, switching hands as it reaches the back of your legs. 0532.json#/instructions/2
  4. As the kettlebell comes forward, pass it to the other hand between your legs. 0532.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue passing the kettlebell between your legs in a figure 8 motion. 0532.json#/instructions/4
  6. Maintain a steady pace and engage your core throughout the exercise. 0532.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0532.json#/instructions/6

0533 — kettlebell front squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0533 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat with a kettlebell held at chest height. The front load builds the quads and glutes and keeps your torso upright.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out. Hold the kettlebell with both hands in front of your chest, close to your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0533.json#/instructions/0, 0533.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold the kettlebell with both hands in front of your chest, close to your body.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0533.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0533.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kettlebell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0533.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out. 0533.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the kettlebell with both hands in front of your chest, close to your body. 0533.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and keep your chest up as you lower your hips down and back, as if sitting into a chair. 0533.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as you can comfortably go. 0533.json#/instructions/3
  5. Drive through your heels to stand back up, squeezing your glutes at the top. 0533.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0533.json#/instructions/5

0534 — kettlebell goblet squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0534 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Hold a kettlebell close to your chest and squat down between your hips. A friendly squat variation for legs, glutes and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell close to your chest with both hands.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0534.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell close to your chest with both hands. 0534.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keeping your chest up and core engaged, lower your body down into a squat position by bending at the knees and hips. 0534.json#/instructions/1
  3. Continue lowering until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as you can comfortably go. 0534.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then push through your heels to return to the starting position. 0534.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0534.json#/instructions/4

0535 — kettlebell hang clean

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0535 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

You swing a kettlebell from a hang position up to the rack at your shoulder in one smooth pull. Trains the hips, back and shoulders for power.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0535.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Use a closed hand contact around the source-defined load or control with wrists neutral. Preserve any grip orientation, hand count, handle position and arm path stated in the phases; unspecified grip/handle geometry remains for review.

authored_functional_contact; source verified: false. The declared equipment requires a control/load contact, but its precise grip geometry is not supplied here. 0535.json#/equipment

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0535.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kettlebell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0535.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your thighs. 0535.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0535.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the kettlebell towards the ground, allowing it to swing between your legs. 0535.json#/instructions/2
  4. Quickly extend your hips and knees, using the momentum to swing the kettlebell up to shoulder height. 0535.json#/instructions/3
  5. As the kettlebell reaches shoulder height, rotate your wrists and catch the kettlebell in the rack position, with your elbow tucked in and the kettlebell resting on your forearm. 0535.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower the kettlebell back down to the starting position between your legs, and repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0535.json#/instructions/5

0536 — kettlebell lunge pass through

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0536 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A lunge where you pass the kettlebell around your front leg at the bottom. Works the legs and glutes while challenging your balance and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your chest with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0536.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your chest with both hands.

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your chest with both hands.

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kettlebell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0536.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in front of your chest with both hands. 0536.json#/instructions/0
  2. Take a step forward with your right foot, lowering your body into a lunge position. 0536.json#/instructions/1
  3. As you lunge forward, pass the kettlebell under your right thigh and transfer it to your left hand. 0536.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push off with your right foot to return to the starting position, while simultaneously passing the kettlebell back to your right hand. 0536.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat the lunge on the opposite side, passing the kettlebell under your left thigh. 0536.json#/instructions/4
  6. Continue alternating lunges and passing the kettlebell between hands for the desired number of repetitions. 0536.json#/instructions/5

0537 — kettlebell one arm clean and jerk

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0537 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

You clean a kettlebell to your shoulder, then dip and drive it overhead with one arm. A full body power move for legs, shoulders and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0537.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0537.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand with an overhand grip.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0537.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Kettlebell. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0537.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, holding a kettlebell in one hand with an overhand grip. 0537.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees slightly and hinge at the hips, lowering the kettlebell between your legs. 0537.json#/instructions/1
  3. Explosively extend your hips, knees, and ankles, using the momentum to swing the kettlebell up to shoulder height. 0537.json#/instructions/2
  4. As the kettlebell reaches shoulder height, rotate your wrist and punch your hand straight up, locking out your arm overhead. 0537.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower the kettlebell back down to the starting position and repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0537.json#/instructions/4

  • Explosively extend your hips, knees, and ankles, using the momentum to swing the kettlebell up to shoulder height. Step 3; 0537.json#/instructions/2
  • As the kettlebell reaches shoulder height, rotate your wrist and punch your hand straight up, locking out your arm overhead. Step 4; 0537.json#/instructions/3

Complete ordered source instructions

  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0550.json#/instructions/5

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A Turkish get up where you come up through a squat style transition while keeping the kettlebell overhead. Trains full body control, stability and strength.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by lying on your back with your legs extended and the kettlebell held in your right hand, arm fully extended above your shoulder.

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Bend your right knee and place your right foot flat on the ground, keeping your left leg extended. 0551.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pressing through your right foot, lift your hips off the ground, coming into a bridge position. 0551.json#/instructions/2
  4. Slide your left leg underneath your body, bending your left knee and placing your left foot flat on the ground. 0551.json#/instructions/3

  8. Reverse the movement to return to the starting position. 0551.json#/instructions/7
  9. Repeat the exercise on the other side, starting with the kettlebell in your left hand. 0551.json#/instructions/8

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0630 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

Proposed staging

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0636.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand up straight with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold an Olympic barbell with an overhand grip. 0636.json#/instructions/0
  2. Let the barbell hang at arm's length in front of your thighs, with your palms facing your body. 0636.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keeping your upper arms stationary, exhale and curl the weights while contracting your biceps. 0636.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue to raise the barbell until your biceps are fully contracted and the barbell is at shoulder level. 0636.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold the contracted position for a brief pause as you squeeze your biceps. 0636.json#/instructions/4
  6. Inhale and slowly begin to lower the barbell back to the starting position. 0636.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0636.json#/instructions/6
